Excel PERCENTILE.INC behavior\n    n=len(sorted_vals)\n    if n==0:\n        return None\n    if k<=0:\n        return sorted_vals[0]\n    if k>=1:\n        return sorted_vals[-1]\n    pos=(n-1)*k + 1  # 1-indexed\n    f=math.floor(pos)\n    d=pos - f\n    if d==0:\n        return sorted_vals[f-1]\n    lower=sorted_vals[f-1]\n    upper=sorted_vals[f]\n    return lower + d*(upper-lower)\n\n# Determine task year columns mapping H-L to years\ncol_letters=[get_column_letter(c) for c in range(8,13)]\ncol_to_year=dict(zip(col_letters, years))\n\ncomputed={}  # coord -> numeric\n\n# lookup areas\nfor (r0,r1) in row_blocks:\n    for r in range(r0,r1+1):\n        code=ws_task[f\"D{r}\"].value\n        for col_letter in col_letters:\n            y=col_to_year[col_letter]\n            computed[f\"{col_letter}{r}\"]=series_to_year_value.get((code,y))\n\n# slack rows\nfor i in range(6):\n    fin_r=12+i\n    scrap_r=19+i\n    cap_r=26+i\n    slack_r=35+i\n    for col_letter in col_letters:\n        fin=computed[f\"{col_letter}{fin_r}\"]\n        scrap=computed[f\"{col_letter}{scrap_r}\"]\n        cap=computed[f\"{col_letter}{cap_r}\"]\n        if fin is None or scrap is None or cap in (None,0):\n            val=None\n        else:\n            val=(fin - scrap)/cap*100\n        computed[f\"{col_letter}{slack_r}\"]=val\n\n# stats rows 42-47\nfor col_letter in col_letters:\n    vals=[computed[f\"{col_letter}{r}\"] for r in range(35,41)]\n    vals=[v for v in vals if v is not None]\n    s=sorted(vals)\n    if not s:\n        minv=maxv=med=avg=p25=p75=None\n    else:\n        minv=s[0]\n        maxv=s[-1]\n        # median for even n: avg of middle two\n        n=len(s)\n        if n%2==1:\n            med=s[n//2]\n        else:\n            med=(s[n//2-1]+s[n//2])/2\n        avg=sum(s)/n\n        p25=percentile_inc(s,0.25)\n        p75=percentile_inc(s,0.75)\n    computed[f\"{col_letter}42\"]=minv\n    computed[f\"{col_letter}43\"]=maxv\n    computed[f\"{col_letter}44\"]=med\n    computed[f\"{col_letter}45\"]=avg\n    computed[f\"{col_letter}46\"]=p25\n    computed[f\"{col_letter}47\"]=p75\n\n# weighted mean row 50\nfor col_letter in col_letters:\n    slack=[computed[f\"{col_letter}{r}\"] for r in range(35,41)]\n    w=[computed[f\"{col_letter}{r}\"] for r in range(26,32)]\n    if any(v is None for v in slack) or any(v is None for v in w) or sum(w)==0:\n        wm=None\n    else:\n        wm=sum(s*v for s,v in zip(slack,w))/sum(w)\n    computed[f\"{col_letter}50\"]=wm\n\n# --- Patch cached values into Task worksheet XML ---\n\ndef fmt_number(v):\n    if v is None:\n        return None\n    if isinstance(v,bool):\n        return '1' if v else '0'\n    if isinstance(v,(int,)):\n        return str(v)\n    if isinstance(v,(float,)):\n        if math.isnan(v) or math.isinf(v):\n            return None\n        # match typical Excel storage; keep reasonable precision\n        return format(v, '.15g')\n    # fall back\n    return str(v)\n\n# Identify Task sheet XML file\nwith zipfile.ZipFile(out_path,'r') as z:\n    workbook_xml=z.read('xl/workbook.xml')\n    rels_xml=z.read('xl/_rels/workbook.xml.rels')\n\nns={'m':'http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main',\n    'r':'http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships'}\n\nwb_tree=ET.fromstring(workbook_xml)\n# map r:id -> sheet name\nsheet_rid=None\nfor sheet in wb_tree.find('m:sheets',ns).findall('m:sheet',ns):\n    if sheet.attrib.get('name')=='Task':\n        sheet_rid=sheet.attrib.get('{http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships}id')\n        break\nif sheet_rid is None:\n    raise RuntimeError('Could not find Task sheet relationship id')\n\nrels_tree=ET.fromstring(rels_xml)\nsheet_target=None\nfor rel in rels_tree.findall('{http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships}Relationship'):\n    if rel.attrib.get('Id')==sheet_rid:\n        sheet_target=rel.attrib.get('Target')  # e.g. worksheets/sheet1.xml\n        break\nif sheet_target is None:\n    raise RuntimeError('Could not resolve Task sheet target')\n\nsheet_path='xl/'+sheet_target\n\nwith zipfile.ZipFile(out_path,'r') as z:\n    sheet_xml=z.read(sheet_path)\n\nsheet_ns='http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main'\nET.register_namespace('', sheet_ns)\nroot=ET.fromstring(sheet_xml)\n\n# Build row->element map\nsheetData=root.find(f'{{{sheet_ns}}}sheetData')\nrows_by_r={}\nfor row_el in sheetData.findall(f'{{{sheet_ns}}}row'):\n    r=row_el.attrib.get('r')\n    if r:\n        rows_by_r[int(r)]=row_el\n\n# helper to find cell element\n\ndef find_cell(row_el, coord):\n    for c in row_el.findall(f'{{{sheet_ns}}}c'):\n        if c.attrib.get('r')==coord:\n            return c\n    return None\n\npatched=0\nfor coord, val in computed.items():\n    r=int(''.join(ch for ch in coord if ch.isdigit()))\n    row_el=rows_by_r.get(r)\n    if row_el is None:\n        continue\n    c_el=find_cell(row_el, coord)\n    if c_el is None:\n        continue\n    vtxt=fmt_number(val)\n    if vtxt is None:\n        continue\n    v_el=c_el.find(f'{{{sheet_ns}}}v')\n    if v_el is None:\n        # insert after formula element if present\n        f_el=c_el.find(f'{{{sheet_ns}}}f')\n        v_el=ET.Element(f'{{{sheet_ns}}}v')\n        v_el.text=vtxt\n        if f_el is None:\n            c_el.append(v_el)\n        else:\n            # find index of f_el\n            children=list(c_el)\n            idx=children.index(f_el)\n            c_el.insert(idx+1, v_el)\n    else:\n        v_el.text=vtxt\n    patched += 1\n\n# write back zip (need to rewrite whole file to preserve other entries)\ntmp_path='/root/output/result_patched.xlsx'\nwith zipfile.ZipFile(out_path,'r') as zin, zipfile.ZipFile(tmp_path,'w',compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zout:\n    for item in zin.infolist():\n        data=zin.read(item.filename)\n        if item.filename==sheet_path:\n            data=ET.tostring(root, encoding='utf-8', xml_declaration=True)\n        zout.writestr(item, data)\n\nos.replace(tmp_path, out_path)\nprint('Patched cached values into', patched, 'cells')\n\nPY","description":"Write formulas to workbook, compute values, and patch cached results into XML","timeout":120000,"run_in_background":false,"dangerouslyDisableSandbox":false}}],"model":"openai/gpt-5.2-20251211","stop_reason":null,"stop_details":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":null,"cache_read_input_tokens":null,"cache_creation":null,"inference_geo":null,"server_tool_use":null,"service_tier":null,"speed":"standard"},"provider":"OpenAI","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1bf52a94-b5f7-4fa2-884d-51d9d9b9e3d7","uuid":"7f06a877-0812-40a3-8e12-0de7f7c47702"}
